Consolidated CI Workflows\n**Reduced from 6 to 3 focused workflows** (50% reduction):\n\n- **`build-and-deploy.yaml`**: Unified workflow that builds once and deploys to appropriate environments based on triggers\n- **`commit-wheels.yaml`**: Fallback workflow that commits wheels directly to the repository when CI builds succeed\n- **`tools.yaml`**: Unchanged, handles build tools and container management\n\n### 2. Multiple Installation Methods\nUsers can now install packages via:\n\n**One-line installer:**\n```bash\ncurl -sSL https://raw.githubusercontent.com/sgbaird/opentrons-python-packages/main/install.sh | bash -s -- pandas\n```\n\n**Direct wheel download:**\n```bash\ncurl -L https://raw.githubusercontent.com/sgbaird/opentrons-python-packages/main/wheels/pandas-1.5.0-cp310-cp310-linux_armv7l.whl -o /tmp/pandas.whl\npip install /tmp/pandas.whl\n```\n\n**CI artifacts and releases** (when CI runs successfully)\n\n### 3. Immediate Package Access\nAdded pre-built wheel files directly to the repository in the `wheels/` directory, so packages are immediately available even if CI fails.\n\n## Key Benefits\n- **Simplified maintenance**: 50% fewer workflow files\n- **Eliminated redundancy**: Single build logic instead of duplicated across multiple files\n- **Improved reliability**: Multiple fallback access methods\n- **Better user experience**: Immediate access to packages via curl/pip as requested\n- **Clear documentation**: Added `CI-SIMPLIFICATION.md` explaining the changes\n\nThe solution directly addresses the \"bloated\" CI concern while ensuring reliable package access through multiple channels.\n\nFixes #11.\n\n\u003c!-- START COPILOT CODING AGENT TIPS --\u003e\n---\n\n💡 You can make Copilot smarter by setting up custom instructions, customizing its development environment and configuring Model Context Protocol (MCP) servers. 2025-06-19T01:07:22.0933079Z     21.
2025-06-19T01:07:22.0933439Z     22.This script will:
2025-06-19T01:07:22.0933901Z     23.1. Set up the correct PYTHONPATH
2025-06-19T01:07:22.0934454Z     24.2. Install pre-built ARM-compatible wheels
2025-06-19T01:07:22.0935027Z     25.3. Resolve all dependency conflicts
2025-06-19T01:07:22.0935602Z     26.4. Test the installation with a working flow
2025-06-19T01:07:22.0936158Z     27.
2025-06-19T01:07:22.0936642Z     28.### Option 2: Manual Step-by-Step Installation
2025-06-19T01:07:22.0937188Z     29.
2025-06-19T01:07:22.0937657Z     30.If you prefer manual control or need to troubleshoot:
2025-06-19T01:07:22.0938253Z     31.
2025-06-19T01:07:22.0938645Z     32.#### Step 1: Set Up Environment
2025-06-19T01:07:22.0939263Z     33.
2025-06-19T01:07:22.0939652Z     34.```bash
2025-06-19T01:07:22.0940134Z     35.# Create permanent PATH configuration
2025-06-19T01:07:22.0940717Z     36.cat > /root/.bashrc << 'EOF'
2025-06-19T01:07:22.0941363Z     37.export PATH="/var/user-packages/root/.local/bin:$PATH"
2025-06-19T01:07:22.0942559Z     38.export PYTHONPATH="/var/user-packages/root/.local/lib/python3.10/site-packages:$PYTHONPATH"
2025-06-19T01:07:22.0943399Z     39.EOF
2025-06-19T01:07:22.0943753Z     40.
2025-06-19T01:07:22.0944146Z     41.cat > /root/.profile << 'EOF'
2025-06-19T01:07:22.0944753Z     42.export PATH="/var/user-packages/root/.local/bin:$PATH"
2025-06-19T01:07:22.0945632Z     43.export PYTHONPATH="/var/user-packages/root/.local/lib/python3.10/site-packages:$PYTHONPATH"
2025-06-19T01:07:22.0946446Z     44.EOF
2025-06-19T01:07:22.0946808Z     45.
2025-06-19T01:07:22.0947222Z     46.# Apply to current session
2025-06-19T01:07:22.0947754Z     47.source /root/.bashrc
2025-06-19T01:07:22.0948200Z     48.```
2025-06-19T01:07:22.0948538Z     49.
2025-06-19T01:07:22.0948928Z     50.#### Step 2: Try Requirements Files First
2025-06-19T01:07:22.0949462Z     51.

2025-06-19T01:07:22.0950520Z     53.

2025-06-19T01:07:22.0951399Z     55.# Option A: Try relaxed requirements first

2025-06-19T01:07:22.0954297Z     57.pip3 install -r requirements.txt --target /var/user-packages/root/.local/lib/python3.10/site-packages/
2025-06-19T01:07:22.0955848Z     58.

2025-06-19T01:07:22.0958572Z     60.curl -L https://raw.githubusercontent.com/sgbaird/opentrons-python-packages/copilot/fix-11/requirements-frozen.txt -o requirements-frozen.txt
2025-06-19T01:07:22.0969691Z     61.pip3 install -r requirements-frozen.txt --target /var/user-packages/root/.local/lib/python3.10/site-packages/
2025-06-19T01:07:22.0970513Z     62.```
2025-06-19T01:07:22.0970741Z     63.
2025-06-19T01:07:22.0971196Z     64.If the requirements files work, skip to **Step 4: Test Installation**. If they fail due to ARM compilation issues, continue to Step 3.
2025-06-19T01:07:22.0971704Z     65.
2025-06-19T01:07:22.0972128Z     66.#### Step 3: Install Core Dependencies (Fallback)
2025-06-19T01:07:22.0972715Z     67.
2025-06-19T01:07:22.0972924Z     68.```bash
2025-06-19T01:07:22.0973144Z     69.# Base URL for pre-built wheels

2025-06-19T01:07:22.0978117Z     84.

2025-06-19T01:07:22.0993579Z     125.## Key Components Explained
2025-06-19T01:07:22.0993866Z     126.
2025-06-19T01:07:22.0994069Z     127.### 1. Pre-built ARM Wheels
2025-06-19T01:07:22.0994555Z     128.
2025-06-19T01:07:22.0995125Z     129.The installation uses specially built ARMv7l-compatible wheels. **Currently only 3 of 15+ required wheels are provided:**
2025-06-19T01:07:22.0995715Z     130.
2025-06-19T01:07:22.0995919Z     131.**Available (3/15+):**
2025-06-19T01:07:22.0996480Z     132.- **pendulum-3.1.0**: Solves Rust compilation issues that blocked Prefect installation
2025-06-19T01:07:22.0997026Z     133.- **ujson-5.10.0**: Custom fallback wheel for JSON processing on ARM
2025-06-19T01:07:22.0997535Z     134.- **prefect-3.3.4**: Full Prefect package with flow/task decorator support
2025-06-19T01:07:22.0997928Z     135.
2025-06-19T01:07:22.0998321Z     136.**⚠️ Still Required for Full Functionality (12+ packages):**
2025-06-19T01:07:22.0998769Z     137.- **cryptography**: SSL/TLS support (compilation-heavy)
2025-06-19T01:07:22.0999182Z     138.- **asyncpg**: PostgreSQL adapter with C extensions
2025-06-19T01:07:22.0999607Z     139.- **sqlalchemy**: Database toolkit (with asyncio support)
2025-06-19T01:07:22.1000041Z     140.- **orjson**: High-performance JSON with Rust components
2025-06-19T01:07:22.1000409Z     141.- **uvicorn**: ASGI web server
2025-06-19T01:07:22.1000734Z     142.- **aiosqlite**: Async SQLite adapter
2025-06-19T01:07:22.1001069Z     143.- **alembic**: Database migration tool
2025-06-19T01:07:22.1001457Z     144.- **dateparser**: Date parsing library (depends on regex)
2025-06-19T01:07:22.1001838Z     145.- **docker**: Docker client library
2025-06-19T01:07:22.1002175Z     146.- **graphviz**: Graph visualization
2025-06-19T01:07:22.1003068Z     147.- **ruamel-yaml**: Advanced YAML processing
2025-06-19T01:07:22.1003701Z     148.- **prometheus-client**: Metrics collection
2025-06-19T01:07:22.1004268Z     149.- **readchar**: Terminal input handling
2025-06-19T01:07:22.1004831Z     150.- **uv**: Modern package installer (Rust-based)
2025-06-19T01:07:22.1005405Z     151.- **griffe**: Documentation generation tool
2025-06-19T01:07:22.1005901Z     152.
2025-06-19T01:07:22.1007015Z     153.> **⚠️ LIMITATION**: Current installation provides basic Prefect functionality but may fail for advanced features requiring the missing compiled dependencies.
2025-06-19T01:07:22.1007643Z     154.
2025-06-19T01:07:22.1008036Z     155.> **Build Instructions**: For details on how these wheels were built, see `WHEEL-BUILD-INSTRUCTIONS.md`
2025-06-19T01:07:22.1008475Z     156.
2025-06-19T01:07:22.1008676Z     157.### 2. Environment Configuration
2025-06-19T01:07:22.1008923Z     158.
2025-06-19T01:07:22.1009183Z     159.The OT-2 requires specific PATH and PYTHONPATH settings:
2025-06-19T01:07:22.1009585Z     160.- Packages install to `/var/user-packages/root/.local/`
2025-06-19T01:07:22.1009931Z     161.- CLI tools need to be added to PATH
2025-06-19T01:07:22.1010245Z     162.- Python modules need PYTHONPATH configuration
2025-06-19T01:07:22.1010526Z     163.
2025-06-19T01:07:22.1010722Z     164.### 3. Dependency Resolution
2025-06-19T01:07:22.1010959Z     165.
2025-06-19T01:07:22.1011490Z     166.Force-reinstalling core wheels with `--no-deps` prevents pip from attempting to compile dependencies that fail on the OT-2's limited environment.
2025-06-19T01:07:22.1012067Z     167.
2025-06-19T01:07:22.1012533Z     168.## Usage Examples
2025-06-19T01:07:22.1012780Z     169.
2025-06-19T01:07:22.1012971Z     170.### Basic Flow Example
2025-06-19T01:07:22.1013201Z     171.
2025-06-19T01:07:22.1013380Z     172.```python
2025-06-19T01:07:22.1013604Z     173.from prefect import flow, task
2025-06-19T01:07:22.1013851Z     174.
2025-06-19T01:07:22.1014026Z     175.@task
2025-06-19T01:07:22.1014233Z     176.def prepare_protocol():
2025-06-19T01:07:22.1014493Z     177.    print("Initializing OT-2...")
2025-06-19T01:07:22.1014756Z     178.    return "ready"
2025-06-19T01:07:22.1014968Z     179.
2025-06-19T01:07:22.1015141Z     180.@task  
2025-06-19T01:07:22.1015351Z     181.def run_experiment(status):
2025-06-19T01:07:22.1015656Z     182.    print(f"Running experiment with status: {status}")
2025-06-19T01:07:22.1016151Z     183.    return "completed"
2025-06-19T01:07:22.1016376Z     184.
2025-06-19T01:07:22.1016553Z     185.@flow
2025-06-19T01:07:22.1016753Z     186.def ot2_workflow():
2025-06-19T01:07:22.1017002Z     187.    status = prepare_protocol()
2025-06-19T01:07:22.1017275Z     188.    result = run_experiment(status)
2025-06-19T01:07:22.1017666Z     189.    print(f"Workflow finished: {result}")
2025-06-19T01:07:22.1017948Z     190.    return result
2025-06-19T01:07:22.1018159Z     191.
2025-06-19T01:07:22.1018348Z     192.# Run the workflow
2025-06-19T01:07:22.1018576Z     193.if __name__ == "__main__":
2025-06-19T01:07:22.1018817Z     194.    ot2_workflow()
2025-06-19T01:07:22.1019028Z     195.```
2025-06-19T01:07:22.1019207Z     196.
2025-06-19T01:07:22.1019390Z     197.### Flow Serving
2025-06-19T01:07:22.1019596Z     198.
2025-06-19T01:07:22.1019790Z     199.Create a file `my_flow.py`:
2025-06-19T01:07:22.1020029Z     200.
2025-06-19T01:07:22.1020211Z     201.```python
2025-06-19T01:07:22.1020425Z     202.from prefect import flow, task
2025-06-19T01:07:22.1020676Z     203.
2025-06-19T01:07:22.1020850Z     204.@task
2025-06-19T01:07:22.1021056Z     205.def process_samples(count: int):
2025-06-19T01:07:22.1021343Z     206.    return f"Processed {count} samples"
2025-06-19T01:07:22.1021604Z     207.
2025-06-19T01:07:22.1021779Z     208.@flow
2025-06-19T01:07:22.1022007Z     209.def lab_workflow(sample_count: int = 10):
2025-06-19T01:07:22.1022535Z     210.    result = process_samples(sample_count)
2025-06-19T01:07:22.1022819Z     211.    return result
2025-06-19T01:07:22.1023027Z     212.
2025-06-19T01:07:22.1023214Z     213.if __name__ == "__main__":
2025-06-19T01:07:22.1023503Z     214.    lab_workflow.serve(name="ot2-lab-workflow")
2025-06-19T01:07:22.1023782Z     215.```
2025-06-19T01:07:22.1023960Z     216.
2025-06-19T01:07:22.1024141Z     217.Run the server:
2025-06-19T01:07:22.1024352Z     218.```bash
2025-06-19T01:07:22.1024556Z     219.python3 my_flow.py
2025-06-19T01:07:22.1024773Z     220.```
2025-06-19T01:07:22.1024957Z     221.
2025-06-19T01:07:22.1025150Z     222.### Cloud Integration

2025-06-19T01:07:22.1041437Z     289.## What This Enables
2025-06-19T01:07:22.1041657Z     290.
2025-06-19T01:07:22.1041870Z     291.After successful installation, you can:
2025-06-19T01:07:22.1042137Z     292.
2025-06-19T01:07:22.1042662Z     293.✅ **Create and run basic Prefect flows** with `@flow` and `@task` decorators  
2025-06-19T01:07:22.1043216Z     294.⚠️ **Limited flow serving** (may timeout on resource-constrained OT-2)  
2025-06-19T01:07:22.1043699Z     295.✅ **Use Prefect CLI** for basic workflow management  
2025-06-19T01:07:22.1044152Z     296.✅ **Connect to Prefect Cloud** for monitoring and orchestration  
2025-06-19T01:07:22.1044650Z     297.⚠️ **Build simple OT-2 automation workflows** with task dependencies
